=== THE MOVE STUDY (onsetstudy.py) · 2023-11-17 -> 2026-11-13 · 737 market days (3.0 years) · 218 shares · 127279 share-days
    tuned on 2023-11-17 -> 2026-05-14 (612 days) · HELD OUT (never used to pick, 18 / 16 Nov included): 2026-05-15 -> 2026-11-13 (125 days)
    illiquid (median real minutes < 340, grid() fills their gaps with the last price - treat with care): none
    shares with 20+ study days in each half-year: 2023 H2 137 · 2024 H1 137 · 2024 H2 172 · 2025 H1 188 · 2025 H2 191 · 2026 H1 205 · 2026 H2 211
    !! MIXED STORE: the early half-years have far fewer shares than the rest (thinner sectors, another universe). Wait for histstore.py
    !! to finish the extension, or run with --years 3. The half-year rule (d) and the tuning / held-out comparison are skewed meanwhile.
    left out as 'not in F&O that day' on 20%+ of their days (a rename missing from ALIAS would look like this): AMBER 395/736, DMART 252/736, ETERNAL 252/736, KALYANKJIL 252/736, NYKAA 252/736, PAYTM 252/736, POLICYBZR 252/736, SWIGGY 278/493, VMM 316/470, BDL 375/736, BLUESTARCO 375/736, CGPOWER 252/736, COCHINSHIP 582/736, DATAPATTNS 724/736, ENRIN 316/346, GVT&D 614/731, KAYNES 375/736, KEI 252/736, MAZDOCK 375/736, NBCC 296/736
    split / bonus-like jumps (an open 20%+ off the last close): 3 on 3 shares - each day and the share's next 20 days left out (63 share-days), so the 20-day volume history (RVOL / RVOL5) never mixes pre- and post-split units: SIEMENS 2025-04-07, VEDL 2026-04-30, TMPV 2025-10-14
    renamed shares read with their old NSE symbol too: ETERNAL (was ZOMATO), TMPV (was TATAMOTORS), UNITDSPR (was MCDOWELL-N), GMRAIRPORT (was GMRINFRA), SAMMAANCAP (was IBULHSGFIN), LTF (was L&TFH)
    sectors.json buckets of unrelated shares, treated as no sector (no breadth / RS): conglomerate, others
READ: CE = the share going up, PE = the share going down - every number below is shown for both. Minute 0 = 09:15. A move is the
day's best run from the OPEN (and, second, from yesterday's close); its ONSET is the first close 0.3% off its base (the day's other
extreme before the peak, its last touch). When the base is the 09:15 candle itself (an OPEN-DRIVE move) the onset is 09:15 by rule.
BEFORE = a ring by the onset + 2 min and before a quarter of the move is done; EARLY = before half is done; LATE = after; MISSED = none.

=== 0. THE ANSWER IN PLAIN WORDS (CE and PE apart · signals picked on the tuning days, numbers over all days unless marked)
  !! the store is MIXED (see the header): read this after the extension finishes, or re-run with --years 3
  CE (the share going UP):
    THE MOVES: 33.9 2%+ moves a day. 64% start AT 09:15 (open-drive) and 22% come with a 1%+ gap (70% one or the other).
      On an open-drive move the onset is 09:15 by rule, so the BEFORE rule (a ring by 09:17) can never count a warning there. But 5%
      of them are a quarter done only at / after 09:30 and 22% break out (BRK) 15+ min after 09:15: a signal can still warn of those,
      graded by timing in 1. So a warning could reach 40% of the 2% moves, not only the 36% that are not open-drive. A move is a
      quarter done 1 min and half done 19 min after its onset; half done 6 min after its breakout minute.
    1 EARLY WARNING: C04 re-arm (DHB + RVOL + FORM)
      BEFORE on 0% of the not-open-drive moves (median lead -22 min on their onset, + = before it) · on the open-drive moves, by
      timing only: before a quarter is done on 0% (1% of those a quarter done only at / after 09:30), no BRK grade (a day-high / low break set: the breakout label is its own shape)
      in all it warns of 0% of the 2% moves in time (0% of those a warning could reach) · early (B+E) 2% · lift 1.75 (95% 1.59-1.90) · +1% before -0.6% 19% vs 11% at random; held out: warns of 0%
      !! even graded by timing on the open-drive moves, no intraday signal warns in time on 10% of the moves a warning could reach (best C04 0%):
      the early warning has to be the pre-open / evening GET READY card with its trigger levels; the intraday signals are the alarm.
    2 THE ALARM / CARD: NO ALARM WITH AN EDGE ON THIS SIDE (needs B+E >= 25%, a lift over 1.2 whose 95% interval (by day) stays above 1.2, 100+ fires, on the tuning days).
      closest by the lift: C20 re-arm (early 1%, lift 1.83, 95% 1.67-2.00, 1354 fires) · the best catch: C04 re-arm (early 2%, lift 1.75, 95% 1.59-1.90) - not a card
    3 REACTION BUDGET: none given - with no alarm that beats random there is no edge for speed to keep (section 5 has the curves).
    4 MOVE LEFT after the alarm: - (no alarm on this side)
    5 RINGS on the CE side, a day median / p90 (the day's busiest 15 min median / p90): early warning C04 re-arm 4 / 8 (15 min 2 / 4) · no alarm · together 4 / 8 (15 min 2 / 4)
    (for comparison, waiting for the sector CONFIRMED: early on 19%, missed 45%)
  PE (the share going DOWN):
    THE MOVES: 37.3 2%+ moves a day. 68% start AT 09:15 (open-drive) and 19% come with a 1%+ gap (72% one or the other).
      On an open-drive move the onset is 09:15 by rule, so the BEFORE rule (a ring by 09:17) can never count a warning there. But 5%
      of them are a quarter done only at / after 09:30 and 22% break out (BRK) 15+ min after 09:15: a signal can still warn of those,
      graded by timing in 1. So a warning could reach 36% of the 2% moves, not only the 32% that are not open-drive. A move is a
      quarter done 1 min and half done 20 min after its onset; half done 7 min after its breakout minute.
    1 EARLY WARNING: C04 (DHB + RVOL + FORM)
      BEFORE on 0% of the not-open-drive moves (median lead -24 min on their onset, + = before it) · on the open-drive moves, by
      timing only: before a quarter is done on 0% (0% of those a quarter done only at / after 09:30), no BRK grade (a day-high / low break set: the breakout label is its own shape)
      in all it warns of 0% of the 2% moves in time (0% of those a warning could reach) · early (B+E) 1% · lift 1.50 (95% 1.31-1.66) · +1% before -0.6% 17% vs 11% at random; held out: warns of 0%
      !! even graded by timing on the open-drive moves, no intraday signal warns in time on 10% of the moves a warning could reach (best C04 0%):
      the early warning has to be the pre-open / evening GET READY card with its trigger levels; the intraday signals are the alarm.
    2 THE ALARM / CARD: NO ALARM WITH AN EDGE ON THIS SIDE (needs B+E >= 25%, a lift over 1.2 whose 95% interval (by day) stays above 1.2, 100+ fires, on the tuning days).
      closest by the lift: C20 re-arm (early 1%, lift 1.72, 95% 1.46-1.94, 909 fires) · the best catch: C04 (early 1%, lift 1.50, 95% 1.31-1.66) - not a card
    3 REACTION BUDGET: none given - with no alarm that beats random there is no edge for speed to keep (section 5 has the curves).
    4 MOVE LEFT after the alarm: - (no alarm on this side)
    5 RINGS on the PE side, a day median / p90 (the day's busiest 15 min median / p90): early warning C04 2 / 6 (15 min 2 / 3) · no alarm · together 2 / 6 (15 min 2 / 3)
    (for comparison, waiting for the sector CONFIRMED: early on 29%, missed 32%)
  RINGS, BOTH SIDES, every set named above: a day median 6, p90 12 · the day's busiest 15 min median 3, p90 5 (the limits: 50 a day, 6 in 15 min)

=== 1. HOW THE MOVES BREAK - 81914 share-day moves of 1.5%+ (from the open) · each side separately · hindsight labels
    READ THE TIMING FROM P25 / P50 / +1%: the ONSET follows the 0.3%-off-the-base rule, so on a range-then-breakout day whose low (high)
    is at 09:15 it reads 09:15, not the breakout. 'od' = open-drive moves (base = the 09:15 candle); BRK = the breakout minute (the last
    fresh break of the day's high / low after 15+ quiet minutes, before half is done; the onset when there is none).

  THE BEFORE CEILING (2% moves): the share that is NOT open-drive - the most the BEFORE rule can count (on an open-drive move the onset
  is 09:15 by rule and BEFORE needs a ring by 09:17, so the rule can never count a warning there). That ceiling is the RULE's: an open-drive
  move a quarter done only at / after 09:30 still leaves time to warn; section 0 grades those by timing (before P25, by the breakout).

=== 4. 18 NOV and 16 NOV REPLAYED - a HELD-OUT check, never used to pick. The population is the old scorecard's: the shares whose close
    moved 2%+ on yesterday's close, that way. The old system's 'before' (35 / 22 of 53) is run_report.py's looser definition (gap opens at
    09:16 counted BEFORE, LATE against a later-found start), so it is set against the study's BEFORE+EARLY, never its strict BEFORE.
  2026-11-18 is not in the store - this check waits for the store to reach it
  2026-11-16 is not in the store - this check waits for the store to reach it

=== 5. REACTION TIME - the same fire entered d minutes later · first fire of the day · TWO populations, never mix them:
    ON THE MOVES IT CAUGHT = only the fires hindsight graded BEFORE / EARLY on a 2% move (an upper bound: what is left on a move that
    really came) · EVERY FIRE = every first fire of that set and side, the population a live ring belongs to (it cannot know the move).
    The EDGE budget is measured on EVERY FIRE against random minutes: the largest d whose HIT60 keeps 90% of its edge over random.
    d = 0 is already the minute's close: a 1-minute candle is known only at t+1:00, so d = 0 means the close + the seconds to read it.
    LEFT = the best price after the entry until 15:10 (an upper bound, a loose bar) · MFE60 = the best move in the 60 min after the entry
    (what a 60-min option trade feels) · HIT60 = +1% before -0.6% in 60 min · OPT / OPT3 = the 60-min option proxy after 1.5% / 3% costs,
    % of the premium
    · CHASE = already 0.5%+ its way since the fire. BUDGET = the largest d that keeps 90% of the d = 0 value (every shorter d too);
    '>= 30 (longest tested)' means it never broke inside the delays tried. Read MFE60 / HIT60 / OPT first: LEFT runs to 15:10.

=== 9. WHAT THIS CAN AND CANNOT SAY
  NO LOOK-AHEAD: every signal at minute t uses that day's minutes 0..t only; the volume history uses only the 20 days before;
    yesterday's futures OI (NSE's day D-1 file) is used on day D, day D's OI never before D+1; the sector and NIFTY only up to t;
    the move labels (onset, breakout, peak, P25 / P50) use hindsight and are never an input (they only grade the fires).
    minutes before a share's first real candle of the day hold its open and no signal fires there (not tradable).
  YESTERDAY is the previous market day (NIFTY's calendar): a share with no full row for it is skipped that day (no older close, high,
    low or OI is used in its place).
  THE ONSET is 'the first close 0.3% off the base': on an open-drive move it is 09:15 and the LEAD / BEFORE of a signal on it is
    measured from the open, not from the breakout. Read P25 / P50 / +1% as the timing; the breakout minute (BRK) is a second label.
  sectors.json is TODAY's sector list - a share that changed sector is in today's. Buckets of unrelated shares (names with 'other',
    'misc', ...) are treated as no sector; a broad real bucket (e.g. 'nbfc & finance') still gives breadth over loosely related shares.
  the F&O list is TODAY's - shares that left F&O are missing; days a share was not in F&O are left out when NSE's file has the day.
    RENAMED shares (ETERNAL<-ZOMATO, TMPV<-TATAMOTORS, UNITDSPR<-MCDOWELL-N, GMRAIRPORT<-GMRINFRA, SAMMAANCAP<-IBULHSGFIN, ADANIENSOL<-ADANITRANS, SHRIRAMFIN<-SRTRANSFIN, LTIM<-LTI, ZYDUSLIFE<-CADILAHC, MOTHERSON<-MOTHERSUMI, LTF<-L&TFH, PVRINOX<-PVR) are read with their old NSE symbol too; a rename not in that list loses its pre-rename
    days (the header lists any share that lost 20%+ of its days this way).
  a weekend FULL session (the 1 Feb 2025 budget day) is a market day like any other; short special sessions are not.
  the store's coverage by half-year is in the header: a store half-way through its 3 -> 5 year extension mixes two universes.
  grid() fills a missing minute with the last price - illiquid shares look smoother than they traded (listed above).
  the 09:15 candle includes the pre-open; circuit days are included.
  SPLITS / BONUSES: whether Kite's minute history is adjusted for them was not checked by hand. A day whose open is 20%+ off the
    share's last close is taken as an unadjusted split / bonus: that day AND the share's next 20 days are left out, because RVOL /
    RVOL5 (the 20-day volume median) and yesterday's high / low would still be in pre-split units (the header lists them; none found
    means the history is adjusted). A split under 20% (e.g. some 5:4 bonuses) is not caught: its volume reads ~1.25x for 20 days.
  only the 20 combinations fixed BEFORE the run were tried (plus 4 unions of them), to limit luck from testing many ideas;
    the winners were picked on the tuning days only and the last 6 months are shown unchanged.
  minute candles hide moves inside the minute: a fast move looks better here than it was to trade (the HFT caveat).
  MOVE LEFT is to the day's best price after the entry - nobody sells the exact top; read it as the room, not the profit.
  the option proxy is the share's move x 0.5 delta on an assumed ~2.5% premium, less 1.5-3% round trip - no option history exists.
  the budget split (data / AI / phone seconds) is assumed, not measured; only the minutes come from the data.
  NIFTY's day type (up / down / mixed) uses the day's close - a split for reading, never an input.
  expiry days: the last Thursday of the month until Aug 2025, the last Tuesday from Sep 2025 (NSE's change), a holiday moves it back;
    a month whose expiry is still ahead of the store's last day is not flagged.
  a fire up to 15 min before a move's base counts for it; earlier fires that day count as MISSED ('rang for an earlier swing').
  18 / 16 Nov are inside the held-out months; they are reported in section 4 only and never decide which set is picked. Their
    population is the old scorecard's (close 2%+ on yesterday's close); the old 35 / 22 'before' is that scorecard's own definition.

(2.0 min · the fires and the moves are kept in /root/hist/movestudy_fires.npz)
